# Painted art: sources and licences

Every image under `public/art/` is listed below with its source, author, licence, original URL, upstream file id and the changes made.
Machine-readable copies: `src/ui/art/painted/manifest.ts` (`PAINTED`: source, author, licence, original URL, changes) and
`scripts/art/recipes.json` (upstream path and id, exact pixel operations). This file is generated from both by
`npx tsx scripts/art/asset-licenses.ts --write`; the same script without `--write` checks that records, files and this file agree.
Images from different licences are never merged into one file; any layering (portrait over a blurred scene, elder tint, masks)
happens in CSS at display time. This is a compliance record, not legal advice.

## Provenance and corresponding source

- Retrieved 2026-10-16 from the `master` branches of the two git repositories and from the pages listed under Sources. Changes (crop, resize, halo clean-up, flattening, WebP re-encoding — listed per file below) were made on 2026-10-16.
- Each Wesnoth and WTactics row names the **git blob SHA-1** of the exact upstream file. A blob id is a hash of the file content, so it keeps
  identifying the original after `master` moves on or the file is renamed or re-encoded: fetch it with `git cat-file -p <blob>` in a clone,
  or from the linked `api.github.com/…/git/blobs/<blob>` URL. All ids were checked against the GitHub trees fetched at download time:
  wesnoth/wesnoth `data/core/images/portraits` tree `0498805975ad6f140cb069c441cc5c35719e184c`, `data/core/images/story` tree `e11278de1343500970528fe81699c4c5392fe4b8`, `data/campaigns` tree `ea3799c8bdd201078c3df6f278965e3fcaab60ee`; `COPYING` blob `3912109b5cd65a68039d473c11c9f7ac2303e06d`, `data/core/about.cfg` blob `558a23e5db3bf5522d5305c6f2eb97b7f6f4314e`;
  wtactics/art root tree `04d860676567986882adc94675da4e2e0a31feb8`. (The commit id of `master` at that moment was not recorded.)
- The "Original" links point at `master` for convenience; the blob id is the stable reference.
- Corresponding source for the GPL v2+ (Wesnoth) images: each WebP file here was made from the upstream file named by its blob id with the
  operations recorded in `scripts/art/recipes.json` (repeated in the Changes column); the originals are lossless WebP/PNG/JPEG files in the
  Wesnoth repository. `node scripts/art/convert.mjs <folder with the originals>` rebuilds every file here byte-for-byte (checked with `--verify`).
- Flare packs: `flareportraitpack.zip` sha256 `e5a9c427d2eea97caf436153aaf805205ba487ca9130b21f895663f725f47328`, `FlareFemales.tar.gz` sha256 `14765984ffb65597ac5662e1ac83cc68ec6361cf37a0c9f032e3e4874acc885d`; per-file sha256 of the extracted PNG below.
  JAP and AIC files: sha256 of the downloaded file below (AIC images are the public 1686px-wide IIIF derivatives).
- (v0.7) v0.7 files were made by the v0.7 art tools (headless-Chrome canvas: crop, paper/backdrop cut-out, sepia/colour tint, resize, WebP). Each recipe with pipeline "v0.7" records the raw file (src, sha256), the exact operations (ops: crop/trim boxes in source pixels, cut, tint, white-out boxes, output size, WebP quality), the change text written by those tools, and the sha256 of the output file. Retrieved 2026-10-16. Archives: `nichol-monsters` [The Free Monsters.zip](https://opengameart.org/sites/default/files/The%20Free%20Monsters.zip) sha256 `2e55e080e3d5038d7618e1a7687215ef1ecd15a0869f2d96ebfddd2a7f882f53`; `nichol-assorted` [Assorted Creatures.zip](https://opengameart.org/sites/default/files/Assorted%20Creatures.zip) sha256 `e64fbdd83401ac741dd7b26efa89af29949cfda56d6be976394b60e20b2b4acd`; `flare2` [PortraitPack2.zip](https://opengameart.org/sites/default/files/PortraitPack2.zip) sha256 `70a1fbe282130410fbdc4574ca1ec07f06144050758f13b33d4908a15933b33a`; `flare3` [PortraitPack3.zip](https://opengameart.org/sites/default/files/PortraitPack3.zip) sha256 `e133d2081be29f96d677f7d27207618be7d0525c5ca35a6c489589f99809b476`; `flare4` [PortraitPack4.zip](https://opengameart.org/sites/default/files/PortraitPack4.zip) sha256 `717358642fe0a54e6cd4beb44914a94f1d06ae64484e9eb5a3764b8d4157c861`; `flare5` [PortraitPack5.zip](https://opengameart.org/sites/default/files/PortraitPack5.zip) sha256 `b6dc893b257599f6fa6a1fa3cff12a4df087a04dfcd80ef770867936a3376f90`. Commons and museum files: sha256 of the downloaded file below (and the Commons SHA-1 in recipes.json).
